Description
Ten questions on the first fifteen arcs: which chapters and episodes each one fills, and which ones exist only in the anime.
Notes for the reviewer
Arc quiz on the first fifteen arcs (007, arc 15). Every question is tagged arc 15 and the quiz level is 15, both scopes. No character is named.

Ten questions on the studio, the composers, the singers of the openings and endings, the comeback of the first opening and the English releases.
Notes for the reviewer
Production and music quiz on arc 0 facts (030, 010, 011, 012, 019, 020, 021, 022). Artists and numbers only, no lyrics, no song titles as distractors; every artist option comes from the theme lists of the facts. Yoshihisa Hirano and Eiichiro Oda come from the facts of other series and this one.
